1782903320773	2026-07-01T10:55:20.773Z	## Environment Assumptions
1782903320773	2026-07-01T10:55:20.773Z	- Expect local users to have `gcloud` installed and configured, including BigQuery access through `bq` or `gcloud` tooling.
1782903320773	2026-07-01T10:55:20.773Z	- Expect local users to be able to retrieve some secrets from Vault when a workflow depends on protected credentials.
1782903320773	2026-07-01T10:55:20.773Z	- Expect key NXG repos to be checked out under the same parent area, typically one or two directory levels above this knowledge-base repo.
1782903320773	2026-07-01T10:55:20.773Z	- If a required repo or tool is missing, state that the standard setup is the preferred path and suggest configuring it rather than silently working around it.
1782903320773	2026-07-01T10:55:20.773Z	- Only fall back to workaround-heavy approaches when the user cannot get the standard tooling or repo layout in place.
1782903320773	2026-07-01T10:55:20.773Z	
1782903320773	2026-07-01T10:55:20.773Z	## Python Tooling
1782903320773	2026-07-01T10:55:20.773Z	- Prefer `uv` over `pip` for Python environment and package management.
1782903320773	2026-07-01T10:55:20.773Z	- If Python work is needed, feel free to create a virtual environment with `uv` or activate an existing one.
1782903320773	2026-07-01T10:55:20.773Z	- Do not default to ad hoc global Python installs when a local virtual environment is available or easy to create.

1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	sympozium-tool — invoke Sympozium tools from a shell.
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	Subcommands (IPC):
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	  send-message   Send a message to a connected channel (Telegram/Slack/Discord/WhatsApp).
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	  schedule       Create, update, suspend, resume, or delete a recurring schedule.
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	  exec           Run a command in a SkillPack sidecar (with the sidecar's RBAC).
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	Subcommands (memory):
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	  memory-search  Search persistent memory for past findings.
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	  memory-store   Persist a finding to memory for future runs.
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	  memory-list    List recent memory entries.
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	Run "sympozium-tool <subcommand> --help" for subcommand options.
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	Usage: sympozium-tool exec [--target NAME] [--workdir DIR] [--timeout SECS] -- <command> [args...]
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	Runs <command> inside the named SkillPack sidecar via /ipc/tools/. The sidecar
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	executes with its own ServiceAccount RBAC (this is how, e.g., the k8s-ops
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	sidecar can call kubectl).
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	Stdout/stderr from the command are printed; this process exits with the
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	command's exit code.
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	Examples:
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	  sympozium-tool exec --target k8s-ops -- kubectl get nodes
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	  sympozium-tool exec --target github-gitops -- gh pr list --repo me/proj
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	  -target string
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	    	SkillPack sidecar to dispatch into (e.g. github-gitops, k8s-ops).
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	  -timeout int
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	    	Timeout in seconds (max 120). (default 30)
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	  -workdir string
1782903320827	2026-07-01T10:55:20.827Z	    	Working directory inside the sidecar. (default "/workspace")

1782903328233	2026-07-01T10:55:28.233Z	## Shared Project-Key Notes
1782903328233	2026-07-01T10:55:28.233Z	- `PM` board hosting does not imply `PM` is the right work-ticket project key.
1782903328233	2026-07-01T10:55:28.233Z	- Boards and Jira project keys do not align one-to-one in the current NXG setup.
1782903328233	2026-07-01T10:55:28.233Z	- `PIP` is the clearest squad-specific project key in the current shared context for Data Platform / ONX Pipeline work.
1782903328233	2026-07-01T10:55:28.233Z	- `ANDC` and `MTS` map cleanly to Android and iOS squad work.
1782903328233	2026-07-01T10:55:28.233Z	
1782903328233	2026-07-01T10:55:28.233Z	## Ticket Routing Rules
1782903328233	2026-07-01T10:55:28.233Z	- Use `ONXD` for `onx-pipeline-dags` DAG and orchestration changes.
1782903328233	2026-07-01T10:55:28.233Z	- Reserve `DAP` for Spark-processing work rather than DAG / orchestration work.
1782903328233	2026-07-01T10:55:28.233Z	- When a ticket is in the wrong Jira project, move the existing issue rather than creating a duplicate and closing the original.
1782903328233	2026-07-01T10:55:28.233Z	
1782903328233	2026-07-01T10:55:28.233Z	## Practical Defaults
1782903328233	2026-07-01T10:55:28.233Z	- If the question is "which board should I inspect?", start with the squad table above.
1782903328233	2026-07-01T10:55:28.233Z	- If the question is "which repo likely goes with this Jira board?", use the same table, then confirm with `../architecture/repo-map.md`.
1782903328233	2026-07-01T10:55:28.233Z	- If the question is "should this be `ONXD` or `DAP`?", treat orchestration as `ONXD` and Spark-processing as `DAP`.

1782903328235	2026-07-01T10:55:28.235Z	## How To Extend This File
1782903328235	2026-07-01T10:55:28.235Z	- Prefer evidence from recent Jira Releases and recent issues inside the project key.
1782903328235	2026-07-01T10:55:28.235Z	- Check which GitHub repos are most often linked from those tickets' PRs.
1782903328235	2026-07-01T10:55:28.235Z	- Promote a mapping from tentative to durable only when the same repo family shows up repeatedly.
1782903328235	2026-07-01T10:55:28.235Z	- Keep dates explicit when adding or changing a mapping because squad boundaries and Jira usage can drift.
1782903328235	2026-07-01T10:55:28.235Z	
1782903328235	2026-07-01T10:55:28.235Z	## Practical Defaults
1782903328235	2026-07-01T10:55:28.235Z	- If the question is "repo X usually lands in which Jira project?":
1782903328235	2026-07-01T10:55:28.235Z	  - start with this table
1782903328235	2026-07-01T10:55:28.235Z	  - then confirm with recent linked PRs on tickets from that Jira project
1782903328235	2026-07-01T10:55:28.235Z	- If the question is "project key Y probably touches which codebase?":
1782903328235	2026-07-01T10:55:28.235Z	  - use this file first
1782903328235	2026-07-01T10:55:28.235Z	  - then `../architecture/repo-map.md`
1782903328235	2026-07-01T10:55:28.235Z	- If the evidence is mixed:
1782903328235	2026-07-01T10:55:28.235Z	  - record multiple likely repos
1782903328235	2026-07-01T10:55:28.235Z	  - say that the mapping is tentative rather than forcing a single answer
